1.1 Three-Domain System Of Classification
Additional	1.	Who proposed the five-kingdom classification system in 1969:
(A)Robert Whittaker(B)Charles Darwin(C)Carl Linnaeus(D)Carl Woese
Additional	2.	Which of the following kingdoms include prokaryotic organisms in Whittaker's system:
